intdb {
	enum CLB_MUX_CTRL {
		C1,
		C2,
		C3,
		C4,
	}

	enum CLB_MUX_X {
		F,
		H,
	}

	enum CLB_MUX_Y {
		G,
		H,
	}

	enum CLB_MUX_XQ {
		DIN,
		FFX,
	}

	enum CLB_MUX_YQ {
		EC,
		FFY,
	}

	enum CLB_MUX_D {
		F,
		G,
		H,
		DIN,
	}

	enum CLB_MUX_CIN {
		COUT_S,
		COUT_N,
	}

	enum CLB_CARRY_ADDSUB {
		ADD,
		SUB,
		ADDSUB,
	}

	enum CLB_CARRY_PROP {
		CONST_0,
		CONST_1,
		XOR,
	}

	enum CLB_CARRY_FGEN {
		F1,
		F3_INV,
		CONST_OP2_ENABLE,
	}

	enum CLB_RAM_DIMS {
		_32X1,
		_16X2,
	}

	enum CLB_MUX_H0 {
		G,
		SR,
	}

	enum CLB_MUX_H2 {
		F,
		DIN,
	}

	enum CLB_FF_MODE {
		FF,
		LATCH,
	}

	enum IO_PULL {
		NONE,
		PULLUP,
		PULLDOWN,
	}

	enum IO_STD {
		CMOS,
		TTL,
	}

	enum IO_SLEW {
		FAST,
		SLOW,
	}

	enum IO_MUX_I {
		I,
		IQ,
		IQL,
	}

	enum IO_SYNC_D {
		I,
		DELAY,
	}

	enum IO_IFF_D {
		I,
		DELAY,
		MEDDELAY,
		SYNC,
	}

	enum IO_MUX_O {
		O1,
		O1_INV,
		O2,
		O2_INV,
		OQ,
		MUX,
	}

	enum IO_MUX_OFF_D {
		O1,
		O2,
	}

	enum IO_MUX_T {
		T,
		TQ,
	}

	enum IO_DRIVE {
		_12,
		_24,
	}

	enum CONFIG_RATE {
		SLOW,
		FAST,
	}

	enum DONE_TIMING {
		Q0,
		Q1Q4,
		Q2,
		Q3,
	}

	enum GTS_GSR_TIMING {
		Q1Q4,
		Q2,
		Q3,
		DONE_IN,
	}

	enum STARTUP_MUX_CLK {
		CCLK,
		USERCLK,
	}

	enum OSC_MUX_OUT {
		F500K,
		F16K,
		F490,
		F15,
	}

	enum OSC_CLK {
		CCLK,
		EXTCLK,
	}

	enum RDBK_MUX_CLK {
		CCLK,
		RDBK,
	}

	enum ADDRESS_LINES {
		_18,
		_22,
	}

	bel_class CLB {
		input F1;

		tile_class CLB {
			cell CELL;
			cell CELL_N;
			cell CELL_E;
			bitrect MAIN: Vertical (rev 36, rev 10);
			bitrect MAIN_S: Vertical (rev 36, rev 10);
			bitrect MAIN_W: Vertical (rev 36, rev 10);
			bitrect MAIN_N: Vertical (rev 36, rev 10);
			bitrect MAIN_E: Vertical (rev 36, rev 10);

			switchbox INT {
				mux CELL.IMUX_CLB_F1 @[MAIN[26][2], MAIN[28][0], MAIN[27][1], MAIN[26][0], MAIN[27][3], MAIN[26][1], MAIN[27][0], MAIN[27][2], MAIN[0][1]] {
					CELL.SINGLE_V[0] = 0b001111111,
					CELL.SINGLE_V[1] = 0b010111111,
					CELL.SINGLE_V[2] = 0b111111111,
					CELL.SINGLE_V[3] = 0b001001111,
					CELL.SINGLE_V[4] = 0b111010111,
					CELL.SINGLE_V[5] = 0b011001011,
					CELL.SINGLE_V[6] = 0b011011001,
					CELL.SINGLE_V[7] = 0b001011101,
					CELL.DOUBLE_V0[0] = 0b111011101,
					CELL.DOUBLE_V0[1] = 0b010010111,
					CELL.DOUBLE_V1[0] = 0b111001111,
					CELL.DOUBLE_V1[1] = 0b011111011,
					CELL.LONG_V[0] = 0b010011101,
					CELL.LONG_V[1] = 0b011010011,
					CELL.LONG_V[3] = 0b010001111,
					CELL.LONG_V[4] = 0b001010111,
					CELL.GCLK[1] = 0b011011110,
				}
				mux CELL.IMUX_CLB_F2 @[MAIN[10][9], MAIN[10][7], MAIN[11][6], MAIN[11][8], MAIN[11][9], MAIN[12][8], MAIN[12][7], MAIN[12][6]] {
					CELL.SINGLE_H[0] = 0b00111111,
					CELL.SINGLE_H[1] = 0b01011111,
					CELL.SINGLE_H[2] = 0b11111111,
					CELL.SINGLE_H[3] = 0b01101111,
					CELL.SINGLE_H[4] = 0b01010011,
					CELL.SINGLE_H[5] = 0b00110011,
					CELL.SINGLE_H[6] = 0b01100011,
					CELL.SINGLE_H[7] = 0b11110101,
					CELL.DOUBLE_H0[0] = 0b11110011,
					CELL.DOUBLE_H0[1] = 0b01010101,
					CELL.DOUBLE_H1[0] = 0b11110110,
					CELL.DOUBLE_H1[1] = 0b00110110,
					CELL.LONG_H[4] = 0b01010110,
					CELL.LONG_H[5] = 0b00110101,
					CELL_N.LONG_H[0] = 0b01100110,
					CELL_N.LONG_H_BUF[2] = 0b01100101,
				}
				mux CELL.IMUX_CLB_F3 @[MAIN[33][2], MAIN[35][0], MAIN[34][2], MAIN[35][1], MAIN[33][0], MAIN[34][1], MAIN[35][2], MAIN[34][0], MAIN[35][3]] {
					CELL.SINGLE_V[0] = 0b000011111,
					CELL.SINGLE_V[1] = 0b011101111,

	tile_slot LLH {

			pass OUT_IO_CLKIN_W = OUT_IO_CLKIN;
		}

		connector_class CNR_NE {
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_N1[0] = DOUBLE_IO_E1[0];
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_N1[1] = DOUBLE_IO_E1[1];
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_N1[2] = DOUBLE_IO_E1[2];
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_N1[3] = DOUBLE_IO_E1[3];
		}
	}

	connector_slot S {
		opposite N;

		connector_class PASS_S {
			pass DOUBLE_IO_E1[0] = DOUBLE_IO_E0[0];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_E1[1] = DOUBLE_IO_E0[1];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_E1[2] = DOUBLE_IO_E0[2];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_E1[3] = DOUBLE_IO_E0[3];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_E2[0] = DOUBLE_IO_E1[0];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_E2[1] = DOUBLE_IO_E1[1];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_E2[2] = DOUBLE_IO_E1[2];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_E2[3] = DOUBLE_IO_E1[3];
			pass IMUX_CLB_F2_N = IMUX_CLB_F2;
			pass IMUX_CLB_G2_N = IMUX_CLB_G2;
			pass IMUX_CLB_C2_N = IMUX_CLB_C2;
			pass OUT_IO_CLKIN_N = OUT_IO_CLKIN;
		}

		connector_class CNR_SE {
		}
	}

	connector_slot N {
		opposite S;

		connector_class PASS_N {
			pass SINGLE_V_S[0] = SINGLE_V[0];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[1] = SINGLE_V[1];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[2] = SINGLE_V[2];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[3] = SINGLE_V[3];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[4] = SINGLE_V[4];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[5] = SINGLE_V[5];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[6] = SINGLE_V[6];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[7] = SINGLE_V[7];
			pass DOUBLE_V1[0] = DOUBLE_V0[0];
			pass DOUBLE_V1[1] = DOUBLE_V0[1];
			pass DOUBLE_V2[0] = DOUBLE_V1[0];
			pass DOUBLE_V2[1] = DOUBLE_V1[1];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W1[0] = DOUBLE_IO_W0[0];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W1[1] = DOUBLE_IO_W0[1];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W1[2] = DOUBLE_IO_W0[2];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W1[3] = DOUBLE_IO_W0[3];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W2[0] = DOUBLE_IO_W1[0];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W2[1] = DOUBLE_IO_W1[1];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W2[2] = DOUBLE_IO_W1[2];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W2[3] = DOUBLE_IO_W1[3];
			pass OUT_CLB_X_S = OUT_CLB_X_V;
			pass OUT_CLB_XQ_S = OUT_CLB_XQ_V;
			pass OUT_IO_WE_I1_S1 = OUT_IO_WE_I1[1];
			pass OUT_IO_WE_I2_S1 = OUT_IO_WE_I2[1];
			pass OUT_IO_CLKIN_S = OUT_IO_CLKIN;
		}

		connector_class PASS_CLB_N_N {
			pass SINGLE_V_S[0] = SINGLE_V[0];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[1] = SINGLE_V[1];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[2] = SINGLE_V[2];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[3] = SINGLE_V[3];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[4] = SINGLE_V[4];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[5] = SINGLE_V[5];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[6] = SINGLE_V[6];
			pass SINGLE_V_S[7] = SINGLE_V[7];
			pass DOUBLE_V1[0] = DOUBLE_V0[0];
			pass DOUBLE_V1[1] = DOUBLE_V0[1];
			pass DOUBLE_V2[0] = DOUBLE_V1[0];
			pass DOUBLE_V2[1] = DOUBLE_V1[1];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W1[0] = DOUBLE_IO_W0[0];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W1[1] = DOUBLE_IO_W0[1];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W1[2] = DOUBLE_IO_W0[2];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W1[3] = DOUBLE_IO_W0[3];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W2[0] = DOUBLE_IO_W1[0];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W2[1] = DOUBLE_IO_W1[1];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W2[2] = DOUBLE_IO_W1[2];
			pass DOUBLE_IO_W2[3] = DOUBLE_IO_W1[3];
			pass OUT_CLB_X_S = OUT_IO_SN_I2[0];
			pass OUT_CLB_XQ_S = OUT_IO_SN_I2[1];
			pass OUT_IO_WE_I1_S1 = OUT_IO_WE_I1[1];
			pass OUT_IO_WE_I2_S1 = OUT_IO_WE_I2[1];
			pass OUT_IO_CLKIN_S = OUT_IO_CLKIN;
		}

		connector_class CNR_NW {
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_W0[0] = DOUBLE_IO_N1[0];
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_W0[1] = DOUBLE_IO_N1[1];
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_W0[2] = DOUBLE_IO_N1[2];
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_W0[3] = DOUBLE_IO_N1[3];
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_W1[0] = DOUBLE_IO_N2[0];
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_W1[1] = DOUBLE_IO_N2[1];
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_W1[2] = DOUBLE_IO_N2[2];
			reflect DOUBLE_IO_W1[3] = DOUBLE_IO_N2[3];
		}
	}
}
